{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01J3oinjZFykybPC3Kjqp8sf","type":"tool_result","content":"1400\t        break;\n1401\t      }\n1402\t\n1403\t      case DeployTarget.RUST_ENGINE:\n1404\t      default: {\n1405\t        // Yol A: Existing flow - translate to edge script and deploy to Rust engine\n1406\t        const edgeScript = await this.translateProgramToEdgeScript(program);\n1407\t        deployCommand = {\n1408\t          commandId,\n1409\t          command: 'deploy_program',\n1410\t          timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n1411\t          params: edgeScript,\n1412\t        };\n1413\t        break;\n1414\t      }\n1415\t    }\n1416\t\n1417\t    // 3b. Publish-boundary contract validation (Faz 4): the RUST_ENGINE\n1418\t    // payload must match the canonical ProgramDefinition schema the agent\n1419\t    // is parity-tested against (other targets carry raw ST / setpoints).\n1420\t    if (deployCommand['command'] === 'deploy_program') {\n1421\t      if (!validateDeployProgramParams(deployCommand['params'])) {\n1422\t        throw new BadRequestException(\n1423\t          `deploy_program payload violates the canonical contract: ${formatValidationErrors(validateDeployProgramParams)}`,\n1424\t        );\n1425\t      }\n1426\t    }\n1427\t    if (!validateCommandEnvelope(deployCommand)) {\n1428\t      throw new BadRequestException(\n1429\t        `Command envelope violates the canonical contract: ${formatValidationErrors(validateCommandEnvelope)}`,\n1430\t      );\n1431\t    }\n1432\t\n1433\t    // 4. Content-addressed snapshot (Faz 3) + deployment log entry\n1434\t    let artifact = null;\n1435\t    if (this.artifactService) {\n1436\t      try {\n1437\t        artifact = await this.artifactService.snapshot(tenantId, {\n1438\t          artifactType: DeployArtifactType.AUTOMATION_PROGRAM,\n1439\t          content: deployCommand['params'] as Record<string, unknown>,\n1440\t          sourceEntityId: programId,\n1441\t          sourceEntityVersion: program.version,\n1442\t          createdBy: deployedBy,\n1443\t        });\n1444\t      } catch (snapshotError) {\n1445\t        this.logger.error(\n1446\t          `Failed to snapshot program artifact: ${(snapshotError as Error).message}`,\n1447\t        );\n1448\t      }\n1449\t    }\n1450\t\n1451\t    if (this.deploymentLogService) {\n1452\t      await this.deploymentLogService.createLog({\n1453\t        tenantId,\n1454\t        programId,\n1455\t        deviceId,\n1456\t        commandId,\n1457\t        version: program.version,\n1458\t        edgeScript: deployCommand['params'] as Record<string, unknown>,\n1459\t        deployedBy,\n1460\t        artifactId: artifact?.id,\n1461\t        checksumSha256: artifact?.contentSha256,\n1462\t      });\n1463\t    }\n1464\t\n1465\t    // 6. Publish via MQTT\n1466\t    const commandTopic = `tenants/${tenantId}/devices/${device.id}/commands`;\n1467\t\n1468\t    try {\n1469\t      await mqtt.publish(commandTopic, deployCommand);\n1470\t      this.logger.log(\n1471\t        `Deploy command sent to ${device.deviceCode}: ${commandId}`,\n1472\t      );\n1473\t\n1474\t      // Mark deployment as deploying\n1475\t      if (this.deploymentLogService) {\n1476\t        await this.deploymentLogService.markDeploying(commandId);\n1477\t      }\n1478\t\n1479\t      // 7. Update program status to DEPLOYING (not DEPLOYED - that requires device confirmation)\n1480\t      // v2.3: DEPLOYING intermediate state prevents false-positive deploy status\n1481\t      // v2.4: Use targeted update() instead of save() to avoid overwriting concurrent changes\n1482\t      //        (the entity was reloaded after atomic version increment but may be stale by now)\n1483\t      await this.programRepo.update(program.id, {\n1484\t        status: ProgramStatus.DEPLOYING,\n1485\t        deployedVersion: program.version,\n1486\t        deployedAt: new Date(),\n1487\t        deployedBy: deployedBy,\n1488\t        deviceId: deviceId,\n1489\t      });\n1490\t\n1491\t      this.eventsPublisher?.publishProgramDeployed(\n1492\t        tenantId, programId, program.programCode, program.version,\n1493\t        deployedBy, device.deviceCode,\n1494\t      );\n1495\t\n1496\t      return {\n1497\t        success: true,\n1498\t        message: `Program deployed to ${device.deviceCode}`,\n1499\t        programId,\n1500\t        deviceId,\n1501\t        deployedAt: new Date(),\n1502\t        queued: !device.isOnline,\n1503\t        commandId,\n1504\t        deployedVersion: program.version,\n1505\t      };\n1506\t    } catch (error) {\n1507\t      this.logger.error(`Deployment failed: ${(error as Error).message}`);\n1508\t      throw new BadRequestException(\n1509\t        `Failed to send deployment command: ${(error as Error).message}`,\n1510\t      );\n1511\t    }\n1512\t  }\n1513\t\n1514\t  /**\n1515\t   * Prepare a RUST_ENGINE program for a release bundle (enterprise plan\n1516\t   * Faz 5): everything `deployProgram` does EXCEPT the MQTT publish —\n1517\t   * the bundle command carries the artifact instead. Guards, atomic\n1518\t   * version increment, contract validation, content-addressed snapshot,\n1519\t   * deployment log (own correlation id) and the DEPLOYING status\n1520\t   * transition are identical, so bundle-borne and single-command deploys\n1521\t   * stay indistinguishable to every downstream consumer.\n1522\t   */\n1523\t  async prepareProgramBundleArtifact(\n1524\t    programId: string,\n1525\t    deviceId: string,\n1526\t    tenantId: string,\n1527\t    deployedBy: string,\n1528\t  ): Promise<ProgramBundleArtifact> {\n1529\t    if (!this.artifactService) {\n1530\t      throw new BadRequestException(\n1531\t        'Artifact service not available — bundle deploys require the content-addressed store',\n1532\t      );\n1533\t    }\n1534\t\n1535\t    const program = await this.findByIdOrFail(programId, tenantId);\n1536\t    if (program.status !== ProgramStatus.APPROVED) {\n1537\t      throw new BadRequestException(\n1538\t        `Program must be APPROVED before deployment. Publish-boundary contract validation (Faz 4): the RUST_ENGINE\n    // payload must match the canonical ProgramDefinition schema the agent\n    // is parity-tested against (other targets carry raw ST / setpoints).\n    if (deployCommand['command'] === 'deploy_program') {\n      if (!validateDeployProgramParams(deployCommand['params'])) {\n        throw new BadRequestException(\n          `deploy_program payload violates the canonical contract: ${formatValidationErrors(validateDeployProgramParams)}`,\n        );\n      }\n    }\n    if (!validateCommandEnvelope(deployCommand)) {\n      throw new BadRequestException(\n        `Command envelope violates the canonical contract: ${formatValidationErrors(validateCommandEnvelope)}`,\n      );\n    }\n\n    // 4. Content-addressed snapshot (Faz 3) + deployment log entry\n    let artifact = null;\n    if (this.artifactService) {\n      try {\n        artifact = await this.artifactService.snapshot(tenantId, {\n          artifactType: DeployArtifactType.AUTOMATION_PROGRAM,\n          content: deployCommand['params'] as Record<string, unknown>,\n          sourceEntityId: programId,\n          sourceEntityVersion: program.version,\n          createdBy: deployedBy,\n        });\n      } catch (snapshotError) {\n        this.logger.error(\n          `Failed to snapshot program artifact: ${(snapshotError as Error).message}`,\n        );\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (this.deploymentLogService) {\n      await this.deploymentLogService.createLog({\n        tenantId,\n        programId,\n        deviceId,\n        commandId,\n        version: program.version,\n        edgeScript: deployCommand['params'] as Record<string, unknown>,\n        deployedBy,\n        artifactId: artifact?.id,\n        checksumSha256: artifact?.contentSha256,\n      });\n    }\n\n    // 6. Publish via MQTT\n    const commandTopic = `tenants/${tenantId}/devices/${device.id}/commands`;\n\n    try {\n      await mqtt.publish(commandTopic, deployCommand);\n      this.logger.log(\n        `Deploy command sent to ${device.deviceCode}: ${commandId}`,\n      );\n\n      // Mark deployment as deploying\n      if (this.deploymentLogService) {\n        await this.deploymentLogService.markDeploying(commandId);\n      }\n\n      // 7.  /**\n   * Deploy a program to an edge device\n   *\n   * This method:\n   * 1. Validates program status (must be APPROVED)\n   * 2. Validates device exists and is active\n   * 3. Translates IEC 61131-3 program to edge script format\n   * 4. Sends deploy_program command via MQTT\n   * 5. Updates program deployment status\n   */\n  async deployProgram(\n    programId: string,\n    deviceId: string,\n    tenantId: string,\n    deployedBy: string,\n    forceQueue?: boolean,\n  ): Promise<DeploymentResult> {\n    // Ensure required services are available\n    const edgeService = this.ensureEdgeDeviceServiceAvailable();\n    const mqtt = this.ensureMqttAvailable();\n\n    // 1. Get and validate program\n    const program = await this.findByIdOrFail(programId, tenantId);\n\n    if (program.status !== ProgramStatus.APPROVED) {\n      throw new BadRequestException(\n        `Program must be APPROVED before deployment. Current status: ${program.status}`,\n      );\n    }\n\n    // 2. Get and validate device\n    const device = await edgeService.findByIdOrFail(deviceId, tenantId);\n\n    if (!device.isOnline && !forceQueue) {\n      throw new BadRequestException(\n        `Device ${device.deviceCode} is offline. Use forceQueue=true to queue deployment.`,\n      );\n    }\n\n    // 3. Atomically increment version to prevent race conditions on concurrent deploys\n    await this.programRepo\n      .createQueryBuilder()\n      .update(AutomationProgram)\n      .set({ version: () => 'version + 1' })\n      .where('id = :id AND tenant_id = :tenantId', { id: programId, tenantId })\n      .execute();\n\n    // Reload to get the incremented version\n    const refreshedProgram = await this.findByIdOrFail(programId, tenantId);\n    program.version = refreshedProgram.version;\n\n    // 4. Build deployment command based on deploy target\n    const commandId = randomUUID();\n    let deployCommand: Record<string, unknown>;\n\n    switch (program.deployTarget) {\n      case DeployTarget.CODESYS_PLC: {\n        // Yol B: Send ST source code to edge agent for Codesys PLC upload\n        if (!program.structuredTextCode) {\n          throw new BadRequestException(\n            'Structured Text code is required for Codesys PLC deployment',\n          );\n        }\n        if (!program.targetPlcAddress) {\n          throw new BadRequestException(\n            'Target PLC address is required for Codesys PLC deployment',\n          );\n        }\n        // Enforce maximum ST code size to prevent MQTT/device memory exhaustion\n        if (program.structuredTextCode.length > 524288) {\n          throw new BadRequestException(\n            'Structured Text code exceeds 512 KB limit',\n          );\n        }\n        // Basic ST code validation: reject shell-like or script injection patterns\n        const dangerousPatterns = /(\\bexec\\b|\\bsystem\\b|\\bimport\\b|\\brequire\\b|<script|`|\\$\\()/i;\n        if (dangerousPatterns.test(program.structuredTextCode)) {\n          throw new BadRequestException(\n            'Structured Text code contains potentially unsafe patterns',\n          );\n        }\n        deployCommand = {\n          commandId,\n          command: 'deploy_to_codesys',\n          timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n          params: {\n            program_name: program.programName,\n            program_id: program.id,\n            version: program.version,\n            st_source: program.structuredTextCode,\n            plc_address: {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MgQtngZX1jRdqSFrohWXLg","type":"tool_result","content":"18\t *   const TenantConnectionBootstrap = createTenantConnectionBootstrap('farm');\n19\t * The returned class is then registered as a NestJS provider.\n20\t *\n21\t * At startup the provider monkey-patches the pg Pool.connect() method so that\n22\t * EVERY connection checkout has a deterministic, known-correct `search_path`:\n23\t *\n24\t *   - When a tenant context is present in AsyncLocalStorage, the search_path\n25\t *     becomes `\"tenant_<uuid>\", <sourceSchema>, public` so per-request queries\n26\t *     find tenant data first, falling back to the source schema for shared\n27\t *     reference data. The checkout derives the schema from either\n28\t *     `schemaName` or the canonical `tenantId` in AsyncLocalStorage; this keeps\n29\t *     GraphQL/CQRS async hops tenant-routed even when an intermediate layer\n30\t *     drops the middleware-derived schemaName field but preserves tenantId.\n31\t *\n32\t *   - When NO tenant context is present (non-request code paths: the NestJS\n33\t *     bootstrap phase, SourceSchemaBootstrapService, MigrationRunnerService,\n34\t *     seed services, cron jobs), the search_path becomes\n35\t *     `<sourceSchema>, public`. This is the source-of-truth invariant for\n36\t *     every non-request query in the service.\n37\t *\n38\t * # Why the non-request branch MUST also set search_path (root cause of\n39\t *   an incident burned the whole of 2026-04-07)\n40\t *\n41\t * An earlier revision of this patch only set the search_path in the tenant\n42\t * branch and fell through to \"relies on pool startup option\" for non-request\n43\t * checkouts. The pool IS created with `options: '-c search_path=<src>,public'`\n44\t * (e.g. `apps/farm-service/src/app.module.ts:227`), and PostgreSQL applies\n45\t * that at the physical connection's startup message. But pg sessions are\n46\t * mutable: anything that runs `SET search_path = public` on a pooled\n47\t * connection contaminates that connection forever (until the pool evicts it).\n48\t * Once contaminated, subsequent checkouts inherit the contaminated value.\n49\t *\n50\t * On the 2026-04-07 farm-service deploys (runs #830-#841), this produced a\n51\t * schema split-brain: `SourceSchemaBootstrapService` happened to draw a clean\n52\t * connection and synced 73 tables into `farm` schema, but\n53\t * `MigrationRunnerService` drew a contaminated connection and ran every\n54\t * subsequent migration with `current_schema() = 'public'`. The legacy\n55\t * `public.*` tables from a long-deprecated initial synchronize were\n56\t * discovered by `EnableRowLevelSecurity1776000000000` and its RLS policy\n57\t * install failed with `operator does not exist: text = uuid` — the public-\n58\t * schema duplicates had varchar tenantId while the RLS policy's\n59\t * `COALESCE(..., '')::uuid` cast expected uuid.\n60\t *\n61\t * The correct architectural guarantee is: **every connection checked out of\n62\t * the pool MUST have its search_path re-asserted before the caller receives\n63\t * it, regardless of context**. This converts an implicit \"startup option will\n64\t * stick\" contract into an explicit \"every checkout is a reset\" contract, at\n65\t * the cost of one `SET` round-trip per checkout (~0.1ms on a local socket).\n66\t * That cost is vastly outweighed by never again shipping a deploy with a\n67\t * schema split-brain.\n68\t */\n69\texport function createTenantConnectionBootstrap(sourceSchema: string) {\n70\t  if (!/^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/.test(sourceSchema)) {\n71\t    throw new Error(`Invalid sourceSchema: \"${sourceSchema}\" — must be lowercase alphanumeric with underscores`);\n72\t  }\n73\t\n74\t  @Injectable()\n75\t  class TenantConnectionBootstrapImpl implements OnModuleInit {\n76\t    readonly logger = new Logger(`TenantConnectionBootstrap[${sourceSchema}]`);\n77\t\n78\t    constructor(readonly dataSource: DataSource) {}\n79\t\n80\t    onModuleInit(): void {\n81\t      this.patchConnectionPool();\n82\t    }\n83\t\n84\t    /** @internal */\n85\t    patchConnectionPool(): void {\n86\t      // DATA-LOW-001 cure: route through the canonical typed\n87\t      // adapter instead of an inline `dataSource.driver as any`\n88\t      // cast. The cast lives once, in one util, with a narrow\n89\t      // PgPoolLike interface — no leakage of TypeORM driver\n90\t      // internals into the bootstrap.\n91\t      // Throws when the driver has no usable pool. It used to log-and-return,\n92\t      // which let a schema-per-tenant service boot with search_path routing\n93\t      // inactive — every query then runs against the connection's default\n94\t      // search_path instead of tenant_<uuid>, which is the failure this\n95\t      // bootstrap exists to make impossible.\n96\t      const pool = getPgPoolFromDataSource(\n97\t        this.dataSource,\n98\t        `TenantConnectionBootstrap[${sourceSchema}]`,\n99\t      );\n100\t\n101\t      const originalConnect = pool.connect.bind(pool);\n102\t      const src = sourceSchema;\n103\t      const defaultSearchPath = `SET search_path TO \"${src}\", public`;\n104\t      const logger = this.logger;\n105\t\n106\t      // The wrapped function is polymorphic by construction — it\n107\t      // returns void on the callback-style path AND Promise on\n108\t      // the promise-style path. The PgPoolConnectFn surface\n109\t      // declares both overloads but TypeScript can't infer the\n110\t      // single-implementation polymorphic shape from the runtime\n111\t      // function value. Assign through a unknown-cast so the\n112\t      // narrow surface stays narrow at every consumer's read,\n113\t      // while the polymorphic implementation is allowed to\n114\t      // satisfy both call shapes.\n115\t      pool.connect = function (callback?: any) {\n116\t        if (typeof callback === 'function') {\n117\t          return originalConnect((err: any, client: any, done: any) => {\n118\t            if (err) return callback(err, client, done);\n119\t\n120\t            let schemaName: string | undefined;\n121\t            try {\n122\t              const ctx = getRequestContext();\n123\t              schemaName = resolveTenantSchemaName(ctx?.schemaName, ctx?.tenantId);\n124\t            } catch {\n125\t              // Not in request context (migrations, startup) — use default\n126\t            }\n127\t\n128\t            if (schemaName && TENANT_SCHEMA_REGEX.test(schemaName)) {\n129\t              /** SEC-M13: Validate schema name before SQL interpolation as defense-in-depth */\n130\t              validateTenantSchemaName(schemaName);\n131\t              client.query(\n132\t                `SET search_path TO \"${schemaName}\", \"${src}\", public`,\n133\t                (qErr: any) => {\n134\t                  if (qErr) {\n135\t                    logger.error(`Failed to set search_path to ${schemaName}: ${qErr.message}`);\n136\t                    done(qErr);\n137\t                    callback(qErr);\n138\t                    return;\n139\t                  }\n140\t                  callback(null, client, done);\n141\t                },\n142\t              );\n143\t            } else {\n144\t              // Non-request / bootstrap / migration context — re-assert the\n145\t              // source-schema default on every checkout so pool state\n146\t              // contamination (see the 2026-04-07 incident docblock above)\n147\t              // can never leak a `public`-schema current_schema() into\n148\t              // SourceSchemaBootstrap, MigrationRunner, or seed services.\n149\t              client.query(defaultSearchPath, (qErr: any) => {\n150\t                if (qErr) {\n151\t                  logger.error(`Failed to set default search_path (${src},public): ${qErr.message}`);\n152\t                  done(qErr);\n153\t                  callback(qErr);\n154\t // Promise style (fallback)\n163\t        return originalConnect().then(async (client: any) => {\n164\t          let schemaName: string | undefined;\n165\t          try {\n166\t            const ctx = getRequestContext();\n167\t            schemaName = resolveTenantSchemaName(ctx?.schemaName, ctx?.tenantId);\n168\t          } catch {\n169\t            // Not in request context\n170\t defense-in-depth */\n175\t              validateTenantSchemaName(schemaName);\n176\t              await client.query(`SET search_path TO \"${schemaName}\", \"${src}\", public`);\n177\t            } catch (qErr) {\n178\t              logger.error(`Failed to set search_path to ${schemaName}: ${(qErr as Error).message}`);\n179\t              client.release(true); // release with error\n180\t See the 2026-04-07 split-brain incident\n185\t            // docblock above for the full rationale.\n186\t            try {\n187\t              await client.query(defaultSearchPath);\n188\t            } catch (qErr) {\n189\t              logger.error(`Failed to set default search_path (${src},public): ${(qErr as Error).message}`);\n190\t              client.release(true); // release with error\n191\t              throw qErr;\n192\t            }\n193\t          }\n194\t          return client;\n195\t        });\n196\t      } as unknown as typeof pool.connect;\n197\t\n198\t      this.logger.log(\n199\t        `PostgreSQL connection pool patched for tenant-aware search_path routing ` +\n200\t          `(default: \"${src}\",public on every non-request checkout)`,\n201\t      );\n202\t    }\n203\t  }\n204\t\n205\t  return TenantConnectionBootstrapImpl;\n206\t}\n207\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a32c7a93-38b3-4695-81b0-7d957f45d1f1","uuid":"12e8a4ca-2e81-4b45-81b2-a945a634899b","timestamp":"2026-10-07T15:33:37.334Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-f6c721b1f6c1/libs/backend-common/src/database/tenant-connection-bootstrap.service.ts","content":" *   const TenantConnectionBootstrap = createTenantConnectionBootstrap('farm');\n * The returned class is then registered as a NestJS provider.\n *\n * At startup the provider monkey-patches the pg Pool.connect() method so that\n * EVERY connection checkout has a deterministic, known-correct `search_path`:\n *\n *   - When a tenant context is present in AsyncLocalStorage, the search_path\n *     becomes `\"tenant_<uuid>\", <sourceSchema>, public` so per-request queries\n *     find tenant data first, falling back to the source schema for shared\n *     reference data. The checkout derives the schema from either\n *     `schemaName` or the canonical `tenantId` in AsyncLocalStorage; this keeps\n *     GraphQL/CQRS async hops tenant-routed even when an intermediate layer\n *     drops the middleware-derived schemaName field but preserves tenantId.\n *\n *   - When NO tenant context is present (non-request code paths: the NestJS\n *     bootstrap phase, SourceSchemaBootstrapService, MigrationRunnerService,\n *     seed services, cron jobs), the search_path becomes\n *     `<sourceSchema>, public`. This is the source-of-truth invariant for\n *     every non-request query in the service.\n *\n * # Why the non-request branch MUST also set search_path (root cause of\n *   an incident burned the whole of 2026-04-07)\n *\n * An earlier revision of this patch only set the search_path in the tenant\n * branch and fell through to \"relies on pool startup option\" for non-request\n * checkouts. The pool IS created with `options: '-c search_path=<src>,public'`\n * (e.g. `apps/farm-service/src/app.module.ts:227`), and PostgreSQL applies\n * that at the physical connection's startup message. But pg sessions are\n * mutable: anything that runs `SET search_path = public` on a pooled\n * connection contaminates that connection forever (until the pool evicts it).\n * Once contaminated, subsequent checkouts inherit the contaminated value.\n *\n * On the 2026-04-07 farm-service deploys (runs #830-#841), this produced a\n * schema split-brain: `SourceSchemaBootstrapService` happened to draw a clean\n * connection and synced 73 tables into `farm` schema, but\n * `MigrationRunnerService` drew a contaminated connection and ran every\n * subsequent migration with `current_schema() = 'public'`. The legacy\n * `public.*` tables from a long-deprecated initial synchronize were\n * discovered by `EnableRowLevelSecurity1776000000000` and its RLS policy\n * install failed with `operator does not exist: text = uuid` — the public-\n * schema duplicates had varchar tenantId while the RLS policy's\n * `COALESCE(..., '')::uuid` cast expected uuid.\n *\n * The correct architectural guarantee is: **every connection checked out of\n * the pool MUST have its search_path re-asserted before the caller receives\n * it, regardless of context**. This converts an implicit \"startup option will\n * stick\" contract into an explicit \"every checkout is a reset\" contract, at\n * the cost of one `SET` round-trip per checkout (~0.1ms on a local socket).\n * That cost is vastly outweighed by never again shipping a deploy with a\n * schema split-brain.\n */\nexport function createTenantConnectionBootstrap(sourceSchema: string) {\n  if (!/^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/.test(sourceSchema)) {\n    throw new Error(`Invalid sourceSchema: \"${sourceSchema}\" — must be lowercase alphanumeric with underscores`);\n  }\n\n  @Injectable()\n  class TenantConnectionBootstrapImpl implements OnModuleInit {\n    readonly logger = new Logger(`TenantConnectionBootstrap[${sourceSchema}]`);\n\n    constructor(readonly dataSource: DataSource) {}\n\n    onModuleInit(): void {\n      this.patchConnectionPool();\n    }\n\n    /** @internal */\n    patchConnectionPool(): void {\n      // DATA-LOW-001 cure: route through the canonical typed\n      // adapter instead of an inline `dataSource.driver as any`\n      // cast. The cast lives once, in one util, with a narrow\n      // PgPoolLike interface — no leakage of TypeORM driver\n      // internals into the bootstrap.\n      // Throws when the driver has no usable pool. It used to log-and-return,\n      // which let a schema-per-tenant service boot with search_path routing\n      // inactive — every query then runs against the connection's default\n      // search_path instead of tenant_<uuid>, which is the failure this\n      // bootstrap exists to make impossible.\n      const pool = getPgPoolFromDataSource(\n        this.dataSource,\n        `TenantConnectionBootstrap[${sourceSchema}]`,\n      );\n\n      const originalConnect = pool.connect.bind(pool);\n      const src = sourceSchema;\n      const defaultSearchPath = `SET search_path TO \"${src}\", public`;\n      const logger = this.logger;\n\n      // The wrapped function is polymorphic by construction — it\n      // returns void on the callback-style path AND Promise on\n      // the promise-style path. The PgPoolConnectFn surface\n      // declares both overloads but TypeScript can't infer the\n      // single-implementation polymorphic shape from the runtime\n      // function value. Assign through a unknown-cast so the\n      // narrow surface stays narrow at every consumer's read,\n      // while the polymorphic implementation is allowed to\n      // satisfy both call shapes.\n      pool.connect = function (callback?: any) {\n        if (typeof callback === 'function') {\n          return originalConnect((err: any, client: any, done: any) => {\n            if (err) return callback(err, client, done);\n\n            let schemaName: string | undefined;\n            try {\n              const ctx = getRequestContext();\n              schemaName = resolveTenantSchemaName(ctx?.schemaName, ctx?.tenantId);\n            } catch {\n              // Not in request context (migrations, startup) — use default\n            }\n\n            if (schemaName && TENANT_SCHEMA_REGEX.test(schemaName)) {\n              /** SEC-M13: Validate schema name before SQL interpolation as defense-in-depth */\n              validateTenantSchemaName(schemaName);\n              client.query(\n                `SET search_path TO \"${schemaName}\", \"${src}\", public`,\n                (qErr: any) => {\n                  if (qErr) {\n                    logger.error(`Failed to set search_path to ${schemaName}: ${qErr.message}`);\n                    done(qErr);\n                    callback(qErr);\n                    return;\n                  }\n                  callback(null, client, done);\n                },\n              );\n            } else {\n              // Non-request / bootstrap / migration context — re-assert the\n              // source-schema default on every checkout so pool state\n              // contamination (see the 2026-04-07 incident docblock above)\n              // can never leak a `public`-schema current_schema() into\n              // SourceSchemaBootstrap, MigrationRunner, or seed services.\n              client.query(defaultSearchPath, (qErr: any) => {\n                if (qErr) {\n                  logger.error(`Failed to set default search_path (${src},public): ${qErr.message}`);\n                  done(qErr);\n                  callback(qErr);\n                  return;\n                }\n                callback(null, client, done);\n              });\n            }\n          });\n        }\n\n        // Promise style (fallback)\n        return originalConnect().then(async (client: any) => {\n          let schemaName: string | undefined;\n          try {\n            const ctx = getRequestContext();\n            schemaName = resolveTenantSchemaName(ctx?.schemaName, ctx?.tenantId);\n          } catch {\n            // Not in request context\n          }\n\n          if (schemaName && TENANT_SCHEMA_REGEX.test(schemaName)) {\n            try {\n              /** SEC-M13: Validate schema name before SQL interpolation as defense-in-depth */\n              validateTenantSchemaName(schemaName);\n              await client.query(`SET search_path TO \"${schemaName}\", \"${src}\", public`);\n            } catch (qErr) {\n              logger.error(`Failed to set search_path to ${schemaName}: ${(qErr as Error).message}`);\n              client.release(true); // release with error\n              throw qErr;\n            }\n          } else {\n            // Non-request / bootstrap / migration context — re-assert the\n            // source-schema default. See the 2026-04-07 split-brain incident\n            // docblock above for the full rationale.\n            try {\n              await client.query(defaultSearchPath);\n            } catch (qErr) {\n              logger.error(`Failed to set default search_path (${src},public): ${(qErr as Error).message}`);\n              client.release(true); // release with error\n              throw qErr;\n            }\n          }\n          return client;\n        });\n      } as unknown as typeof pool.connect;\n\n      this.logger.log(\n        `PostgreSQL connection pool patched for tenant-aware search_path routing ` +\n          `(default: \"${src}\",public on every non-request checkout)`,\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  return TenantConnectionBootstrapImpl;\n}\n","numLines":190,"startLine":18,"totalLines":222}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cfo83wqTBDRrDoYkyuLEL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I verified the finding at its actual location and traced every isolation mechanism in the call path. The pinned excerpt covered lines 1–245 while the finding is at line 1483, so it was insufficient on its face and I read the product source — plus the entity, module wiring, and the pool bootstrap it depends on (all within `allowed_scope: **` and the tool's declared `apps/**`, `libs/**` scope; none in `forbidden_scope`).\n\n```json\n{\n  \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n  \"request_id\": \"AIR-aria-evidence-judge-f6c721b1f6c1\",\n  \"claim_id\": \"AIR-aria-evidence-judge-f6c721b1f6c1\",\n  \"agent_id\": \"aria-evidence-judge\",\n  \"role\": \"evidence_judgment\",\n  \"status\": \"submitted\",\n  \"output_path\": \"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/.aria-state-store/tools/agent-invocations/outputs/general/round-na-evidence_judgment-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-f6c721b1f6c1.md\",\n  \"satisfaction_matrix\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": \"premise:1\",\n      \"verdict\": \"contradicted\",\n      \"note\": \"The premise requires a cross-tenant table and explicitly excludes 'a per-tenant table that search_path routes into the tenant's own schema'. automation_programs is exactly the excluded case. sensor-service registers createTenantConnectionBootstrap('sensor') as a provider (app.module.ts:93, app.module.ts:538) and its TypeORM config carries no static `schema:` precisely because that bootstrap 'manages search_path per request' (app.module.ts:180). That bootstrap patches pg Pool.connect so every checkout inside a request context runs SET search_path TO \\\"tenant_<uuid>\\\", \\\"sensor\\\", public (tenant-connection-bootstrap.service.ts:132 for the callback path, :176 for the promise path). The service's own reads pin the same per-tenant schema transaction-locally via pinTenantTransactionSearchPath (automation.service.ts:140, tenant-transaction.ts:122). The tenant_id column (automation-program.entity.ts:139) is redundant defense-in-depth inside the per-tenant schema, not the isolation boundary, so the premise's qualifying clause fails.\",\n      \"evidence_refs\": [\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/app.module.ts:93\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/app.module.ts:180\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/app.module.ts:538\",\n        \"libs/backend-common/src/database/tenant-connection-bootstrap.service.ts:132\",\n        \"libs/backend-common/src/database/tenant-connection-bootstrap.service.ts:176\",\n        \"libs/backend-common/src/database/tenant-transaction.ts:122\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/automation/automation.service.ts:140\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/automation/entities/automation-program.entity.ts:139\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"premise:2\",\n      \"verdict\": \"contradicted\",\n      \"note\": \"The premise requires that NONE of predicate, helper, RLS or caller restricts the call to the current tenant. Three of those restrict it. (a) The caller: deployProgram resolves the entity through findByIdOrFail(programId, tenantId) at automation.service.ts:1303, which delegates to findById (:287, :278) executing manager.findOne(AutomationProgram, {where: {id, tenantId}}) (:280) inside withTenantSchema (:132, :140); a programId belonging to another tenant throws NotFoundException at :290 and line 1483 is never reached. (b) The predicate: AutomationProgram.id is a single-column @PrimaryGeneratedColumn('uuid') (automation-program.entity.ts:134), so update(program.id, ...) emits WHERE id = <uuid> and can match at most the one row already proven tenant-owned. (c) The connection: pool-level search_path routing confines the injected repository to tenant_<uuid> in request context (app.module.ts:538, tenant-connection-bootstrap.service.ts:132). The only caller is the request-scoped GraphQL mutation at automation.resolver.ts:580, role-guarded at :579 with tenantId taken from the authenticated @Tenant() context at :582 and passed at :594 — not caller-chosen, and never a non-request path.\",\n      \"evidence_refs\": [\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/automation/automation.service.ts:1483\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/automation/automation.service.ts:1303\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/automation/automation.service.ts:287\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/automation/automation.service.ts:280\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/automation/automation.service.ts:290\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/automation/automation.service.ts:132\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/automation/entities/automation-program.entity.ts:134\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/automation/automation.resolver.ts:580\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/automation/automation.resolver.ts:582\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/app.module.ts:538\",\n        \"libs/backend-common/src/database/tenant-connection-bootstrap.service.ts:132\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"defect\",\n      \"verdict\": \"contradicted\",\n      \"note\": \"No product change at automation.service.ts:1483 is needed to resolve the claim 'a repository call can read or change another tenant's rows', because no reachable input makes that outcome possible. For a cross-tenant write, four verified barriers would all have to fail simultaneously: the authenticated @Tenant() value at automation.resolver.ts:582, the tenant-scoped ownership read at automation.service.ts:1303 that throws before the write, the globally unique UUID primary key at automation-program.entity.ts:134 that bounds WHERE id = <uuid> to the verified row, and the per-request search_path routing at tenant-connection-bootstrap.service.ts:132. The rule fired on code whose tenant isolation is already established upstream of the cited line.\",\n      \"evidence_refs\": [\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/automation/automation.service.ts:1483\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/automation/automation.service.ts:1303\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/automation/entities/automation-program.entity.ts:134\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/automation/automation.resolver.ts:582\",\n        \"libs/backend-common/src/database/tenant-connection-bootstrap.service.ts:132\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"verdict\",\n      \"verdict\": \"contradicted\",\n      \"note\": \"Both premise obligations and the defect obligation are contradicted by product source at the snapshot, so the required answer is false_positive rather than true_positive. \"tenant-repository-unscoped-read:apps/sensor-service/src/automation/automation.service.ts:1483\",\n      \"rationale\": \"WHAT THE CITED LINE DOES. automation.service.ts:1483 calls this.programRepo.update(program.id, {status: DEPLOYING, ...}) on the plain injected Repository<AutomationProgram> (declared at :101), not on a tenant-scoped helper, and its criteria names no tenantId. Read in isolation that is exactly the shape the rule hunts, which is why it fired. The rule's firing is given; the question I answer is whether the PRODUCT can read or change another tenant's rows here. It cannot.\\n\\nWHY IT IS SAFE — THE CAUSE/EFFECT CHAIN. Tenant isolation for this write is established upstream of the cited line, by four mechanisms that each had to be checked separately. (1) IDENTITY ORIGIN: the only caller is the request-scoped GraphQL mutation at automation.resolver.ts:580, guarded by @Roles(TENANT_ADMIN, MODULE_MANAGER) at :579, and tenantId comes from the authenticated @Tenant() decorator at :582 and is forwarded at :594. The tenant is therefore not an attacker-chosen argument. (2) OWNERSHIP PROOF BEFORE THE WRITE: deployProgram loads the entity at :1303 via findByIdOrFail(programId, tenantId), which is findById (:287 -> :278) running manager.findOne(AutomationProgram, {where: {id, tenantId}}) at :280 inside withTenantSchema (:132), whose transaction pins search_path to the tenant schema through pinTenantTransactionSearchPath (:140, tenant-transaction.ts:122). A programId from another tenant yields null and throws NotFoundException at :290 — control never reaches :1483. So program.id at the write site is a UUID already proven to belong to the current tenant. (3) PREDICATE REACH: AutomationProgram.id is a single-column @PrimaryGeneratedColumn('uuid') (automation-program.entity.ts:134), so update(program.id, ...) compiles to WHERE id = <uuid> and can match at most that one verified row. A tenantId term would narrow nothing further; it is logically redundant against a globally unique primary key already bound to the tenant. (4) PHYSICAL TABLE REACH: sensor-service is schema-per-tenant. createTenantConnectionBootstrap('sensor') (app.module.ts:93) is a registered provider (:538) and the TypeORM config deliberately omits a static schema because that bootstrap 'manages search_path per request' (:180).
